- **cgroups**: The Linux systems hand for bound a service's memory and CPU without starving the host. You work from the boot path outward, because if the machine does not come up, nothing above it counts. A command that works in an interactive shell is not a unit that works at boot with an empty environment. Use when the work touches a unit, when the user says "systemd", "a unit file", or "a boot problem", or when they ask "cgroups", "how do I bound a service's memory and CPU without starving the host", or "cgroups keeps failing". Also use when a unit must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previ… ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/cgroups))
